# EXAMPLES — prompt templates for ai-visual-artist

Prompt templates for Time Optic (posts, carousels, Reels, story clips, comics, promo images). Each block uses only the fixed ids from `research/_BRIEF.md` (composition, lighting, the 102 art styles, emotion ids and palette ids), so it can be pasted straight into a Visual Job (`VISUAL_QUALITY_SCHEMA.json`).

How to read a block:

- **Header** `[purpose · aspect · style_id · composition_id · lighting_id · emotion_id · palette]`.
- **Template** has `{slots}`; **Filled** is one filled version for this project. All prompts are text-free: Thai/English text, prices and logos go in the HTML layer (`npm run render`).
- **Avoid** lists what to keep out. For FLUX (no negative prompt at CFG 1) these items are already written into the prompt as a positive description; for Codex you may also say them plainly.
- These are tools, not formulas (`Artistic`): every composition/lighting/style choice here has alternatives in its library. No living artist names, studio brands or copyrighted characters appear, and none should be added.
- Product rule: the real frame photo is the truth. Either Codex with the photo attached (`-i`) and "keep the frame exactly as in the photo", or generate an empty scene and composite the real cutout from `kits/` in HTML. Every Codex run costs the user's quota, so ask first.

- Default provider is FLUX schnell local unless the header block's notes say Codex.


## (a) Time Optic product & promo (the real frame photo stays unchanged)

**T001** [product_ad · 1:1 · photoreal_product · rule_of_thirds · eyewear_lens_reflection_control · will.confidence · pal.brand_timeoptic]  
Goal: Hero shot of the real frame that a customer would recognise in the shop.  
Template: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. The {frame} from the photo rests open on {surface}, temples unfolded, seen from a slightly high three-quarter angle. A soft green-tinted shadow falls behind it and the background fades to {bg_colour}. Photoreal product photography, sharp focus on the product, accurate materials, clean controlled reflections. Eyewear lighting: large diffused sources placed off-axis so the lenses show only a soft gradient reflection with no hot spots, and what is behind the lenses stays visible. The main subject sits on a third-line intersection and the opposite two thirds stay calm. The mood is confident and assured: upright forms, clean edges, steady contrast. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. The round thin gold metal frame from the photo rests open on a slab of pale honed travertine, temples unfolded, seen from a slightly high three-quarter angle. A soft green-tinted shadow falls behind it and the background fades to deep phthalo green. Photoreal product photography, sharp focus on the product, accurate materials, clean controlled reflections. Eyewear lighting: large diffused sources placed off-axis so the lenses show only a soft gradient reflection with no hot spots, and what is behind the lenses stays visible. The main subject sits on a third-line intersection and the opposite two thirds stay calm. The mood is confident and assured: upright forms, clean edges, steady contrast. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Avoid: redesigned bridge or nose pads, extra screws or rivets, mirrored hot spots on the lenses, warped temples, fake logos.  
Notes: Upper-left third stays empty for the HTML headline; partner logo goes top-left in HTML. Codex (ask the user first, it uses the ChatGPT quota): attach the real frame photo with -i and say "keep the frame exactly as in the photo"; compare shape, colour, bridge, hinges and temple tips with the photo, and if anything drifts composite the real cutout in HTML instead.

**T002** [product_ad · 4:5 · photoreal_product · negative_space · product_softbox · performance.trusted_expert · pal.brand_timeoptic]  
Goal: Empty premium set where the real cutout of the frame is composited later.  
Template: An empty {surface} under a soft overhead light, with a {prop} at the far right edge. The centre-left area of the surface is clean and empty, sized for a pair of glasses that will be placed later, with a soft contact-shadow zone. Photoreal product photography, sharp focus on the product, accurate materials, clean controlled reflections. A large overhead softbox, clean even product light, soft graduated reflections. The subject is small and a large empty area surrounds it. The mood is trustworthy and expert: tidy, well lit, calm neutral colour, precise detail. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: An empty matte timberwolf-beige plinth against a snow-white wall under a soft overhead light, with a small sprig of dried eucalyptus at the far right edge. The centre-left area of the surface is clean and empty, sized for a pair of glasses that will be placed later, with a soft contact-shadow zone. Photoreal product photography, sharp focus on the product, accurate materials, clean controlled reflections. A large overhead softbox, clean even product light, soft graduated reflections. The subject is small and a large empty area surrounds it. The mood is trustworthy and expert: tidy, well lit, calm neutral colour, precise detail. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Avoid: any glasses or eyewear drawn by the model, props in the centre, clutter, harsh shadows.  
Notes: Composite `kits/<frame>/cutouts/*.png` in HTML; add a soft CSS drop shadow matching the light from the top. FLUX schnell local (free, no quota): keep natural sentences of about 60-120 words, no negative prompt at CFG 1, so the Avoid list is written into the prompt as positive description; for layout control block the shapes out in HTML and run img2img (--init layout.png --denoise 0.55-0.75).

**T003** [product_ad · 1:1 · macro_photography · subject_isolation · jewelry_specular · cognitive.interest · pal.brand_timeoptic]  
Goal: Detail punch-in that proves craftsmanship (hinge, temple tip, bridge).  
Template: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. An extreme close-up of the {detail} of the {frame} from the photo, the focus plane on the metal edge, the rest melting into a creamy {bg} blur. Macro photography, extreme close detail, razor-thin focus plane, creamy blur. Small hard sources making crisp sparkling highlights on polished metal. The subject is isolated against a simple background with shallow focus or a clean tone. The mood is interested: one clear detail lit and sharp, everything else quiet. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. An extreme close-up of the five-barrel hinge of the black acetate frame from the photo, the focus plane on the metal edge, the rest melting into a creamy warm grey blur. Macro photography, extreme close detail, razor-thin focus plane, creamy blur. Small hard sources making crisp sparkling highlights on polished metal. The subject is isolated against a simple background with shallow focus or a clean tone. The mood is interested: one clear detail lit and sharp, everything else quiet. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Avoid: invented engravings, extra hinge barrels, changed acetate colour, dust and fingerprints.  
Notes: Macro shots drift most: check the hinge count and the acetate pattern against the photo before use. **T005** [product_ad · 4:5 · photoreal_lifestyle · golden_ratio · window_side · joy.contentment · pal.brand_timeoptic]  
Goal: A person wearing the real frame in a believable Thai daily moment.  
Template: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. A {person} sits by a café window in {place}, reading a paperback, wearing the {frame} from the photo. The glasses sit level on the nose with natural reflections; the background is softly out of focus. Photoreal lifestyle photography, natural candid moment, real environment, gentle depth of field. Soft side light from a nearby window, gradual falloff into shadow. The subject sits at about the 0.62 division of the frame, the larger part left as breathing room. The mood is content: soft warm light, settled horizontal shapes, nothing pulling for attention. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. The glasses sit level on the nose with natural reflections; the background is softly out of focus. Photoreal lifestyle photography, natural candid moment, real environment, gentle depth of field. Soft side light from a nearby window, gradual falloff into shadow. The subject sits at about the 0.62 division of the frame, the larger part left as breathing room. The mood is content: soft warm light, settled horizontal shapes, nothing pulling for attention. Notes: Attach both the frame photo and, if the person is real staff, their photo with -i; ask before every run. **T010** [product_ad · 4:5 · cinematic_realism · split_screen · screen_glow_face · will.urgency · pal.brand_timeoptic]  
Goal: Blue-light coating post: before/after of tired eyes at night.  
Template: Two halves of the same desk at night. Left: a {person} rubbing tired eyes beside a laptop glow. Right: the same person relaxed and focused, a pair of plain {frame} on their face. Cinematic realism, film-like colour grade, wide-screen feel, motivated light, shallow depth of field. Cool screen glow lighting the face from below and in front in a dark room. The frame divides into two equal halves that contrast with each other. The mood is urgent: tilted lines, tight framing, sharp contrast. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: Two halves of the same desk at night. Left: a young office worker rubbing tired eyes beside a laptop glow. Right: the same person relaxed and focused, a pair of plain thin black glasses on their face. Cinematic realism, film-like colour grade, wide-screen feel, motivated light, shallow depth of field. Cool screen glow lighting the face from below and in front in a dark room. The frame divides into two equal halves that contrast with each other. The mood is urgent: tilted lines, tight framing, sharp contrast. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Avoid: screen content, keyboard letters, different person on each side.  
Notes: Build two FLUX images with the same seed and composite as a split in HTML; the frame on the right is a placeholder, so for a real product run Codex with -i. FLUX schnell local (free, no quota): keep natural sentences of about 60-120 words, no negative prompt at CFG 1, so the Avoid list is written into the prompt as positive description; for layout control block the shapes out in HTML and run img2img (--init layout.png --denoise 0.55-0.75).

**T038** [reel_frame · 9:16 · cinematic_realism · center · eyewear_lens_reflection_control · cognitive.curiosity · pal.brand_timeoptic]  
Goal: Hook frame: the real frame appears in the first second.  
Template: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. The {frame} from the photo hangs in mid-air at the centre of a {bg}, slightly turned, the lenses catching a soft gradient. Cinematic realism, film-like colour grade, wide-screen feel, motivated light, shallow depth of field. Eyewear lighting: large diffused sources placed off-axis so the lenses show only a soft gradient reflection with no hot spots, and what is behind the lenses stays visible. The subject sits dead centre with even space around it. The mood is curious: a half-revealed detail, a path that invites the eye inward. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: Use the attached photo of the glasses and keep the frame exactly as in the photo: same shape, colour, bridge, hinges, temples and any logo, nothing redesigned. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.

Template: A style study of {subject}, rendered entirely in this medium so that the technique itself carries the image. Precise botanical watercolour, layered glazes, crisp dry-brush detail on clean white paper. Soft side light from a nearby window, gradual falloff into shadow. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.

**T166** [other · 4:5 · storybook_gouache · golden_ratio · golden_hour · joy.contentment · custom (#F2C57C #8C6A4F #F4EDE1)]  
Goal: Translate joy.contentment into shape, value, colour and light, not just a facial expression.  
Template: An emotion study: {subject}. Storybook gouache painting, matte opaque colour, soft rounded shapes, gentle brush texture on warm paper. Golden-hour sun, low warm light, long soft shadows, glowing edges. The subject sits at about the 0.62 division of the frame, the larger part left as breathing room. The mood is content: soft warm light, settled horizontal shapes, nothing pulling for attention. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: An emotion study: an old shopkeeper sipping tea on the shop's front step. Storybook gouache painting, matte opaque colour, soft rounded shapes, gentle brush texture on warm paper. Golden-hour sun, low warm light, long soft shadows, glowing edges. The subject sits at about the 0.62 division of the frame, the larger part left as breathing room. The mood is content: soft warm light, settled horizontal shapes, nothing pulling for attention. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Avoid: clichés that contradict the light (a smiling face in a gloomy grade), text, extra figures.  
Notes: Emotion is carried by value range, temperature and space first, then by pose; change one of these at a time when iterating. ## (k) Lighting studies

**T188** [other · 4:5 · photoreal_lifestyle · rule_of_thirds · broad · performance.trusted_expert · custom]  
Goal: Show exactly what broad looks like on a typical project subject.  
Template: A lighting study of {subject}. Photoreal lifestyle photography, natural candid moment, real environment, gentle depth of field. Broad lighting, the side of the face turned toward the camera is lit, open and friendly. The main subject sits on a third-line intersection and the opposite two thirds stay calm. The mood is trustworthy and expert: tidy, well lit, calm neutral colour, precise detail. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Filled: A lighting study of a head-and-shoulders portrait of a Thai man in his forties wearing thin metal glasses. Photoreal lifestyle photography, natural candid moment, real environment, gentle depth of field. Broad lighting, the side of the face turned toward the camera is lit, open and friendly. The main subject sits on a third-line intersection and the opposite two thirds stay calm. The mood is trustworthy and expert: tidy, well lit, calm neutral colour, precise detail. All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Avoid: extra light sources that contradict the setup, blown highlights, text.  
Notes: Lighting study: keep subject, lens and framing fixed and change only the light, so the comparison is fair. With glasses on a face, move the key until its reflection leaves the eyes visible. FLUX schnell local (free, no quota): keep natural sentences of about 60-120 words, no negative prompt at CFG 1, so the Avoid list is written into the prompt as positive description; for layout control block the shapes out in HTML and run img2img (--init layout.png --denoise 0.55-0.75).

All surfaces stay clean and free of lettering, signs or logos; any words are added later in a separate layer.  
Avoid: extra light sources that contradict the setup, blown highlights, text.  
Notes: Lighting study: keep subject, lens and framing fixed and change only the light, so the comparison is fair. With glasses on a face, move the key until its reflection leaves the eyes visible. FLUX schnell local (free, no quota): keep natural sentences of about 60-120 words, no negative prompt at CFG 1, so the Avoid list is written into the prompt as positive description; for layout control block the shapes out in HTML and run img2img (--init layout.png --denoise 0.55-0.75).
